HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_113534271880f72e1e41","text":"3. **The visible human forgetting the attached human.** `ء ن س:B001` supplies visible human presence, while the window retains the human’s origin from clinging `علق` (96:2) and immediately gives reflexive sight (96:7). The fixed `الإنسان` is thus both socially manifest and materially dependent. I infer that prominence in the visible scene helps conceal the dependence from which it arose. The line reads as an irony of human presence: becoming conspicuous can support the illusion that one is unconditioned.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_16f8886c28736ab1edf7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:6:3\",\"root_000059\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Toplum önünde görünür hâle gelen insan, bağlı kökenini unutunca görünürlüğünü koşulsuz varlık sanabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1a85dc9fff51bab97d76","text":"96:19 made the transgressor's problem not just excess but attempted obedience-capture. The later “do not obey him” reveals that `ليطغى` has already begun to form an alternative authority.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B001","root_000937/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2560aa6eaa3e30ad9ade","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:6:4\",\"root_000937\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Taşkınlık, özel bir kibir olarak kalmaz; başkasının ibadetini denetleyen ve itaati kendine çekmeye çalışan sahte bir otoriteye dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2f878e47fbaef9a75c18","text":"96:7 supplied the immediate hydraulic trigger: abundance becomes overflow when the receiver judges it self-sufficient. More surprisingly, 96:9–11 gave `ط غ ي:B003` a concrete social body in the person who blocks a praying servant positioned on guidance. The later ayat corrected a merely private vice-reading into one of rival authority exercised over another.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B001","root_000937/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_301a8e968c342e776a64","text":"1. **Received abundance becoming overflow.** `ط غ ي:B002` supplies water or force rising beyond measure and overwhelming its bounds; `ط غ ي:B001` supplies the corresponding moral overstep. The line follows creation, generosity, pen, and teaching, then 96:7 identifies a self-sufficient self-perception. The overflow image makes received capacity the “water” that exceeds its channel when treated as self-owned. The packet does not state that teaching causes rebellion; I infer that abundance without acknowledged dependence enables the excess. `ليطغى` consequently reads as gifted power spilling beyond its proper containment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000937/B001","root_000937/B002","root_000937/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5b33c135a7e5ae075c07","text":"1. Human transgression is an overflow beyond measured createdness. Evidence: `ء ن س B001` visible human presence; `ط غ ي B001` overstepping the bound in rebellion; `ط غ ي B002` overflowing/overwhelming force; earlier `خ ل ق B001/B003` measuring and well-proportioned form. Structural cue: the first `كلا` breaks the teaching sequence and introduces a reversal: the taught human exceeds bounds. Mechanism: the human formed by measure behaves like water or force rising beyond measure. Abductive move: the packet supplies measured creation and overflow branches; I infer that transgression is a distortion of created proportion. Reading change: `ليطغى` reads as both moral rebellion and flood-like excess. 2. `طغى` activates false authority. Evidence: `ط غ ي B003` ṭāghūt as false authority/object of worship; `ء ل ه B001` worship and worshipped one; later 96:19 `ط و ع B001` obedience. Structural cue: after the human exceeds bounds, later the addressee is told not to obey him. Mechanism: overstepping becomes a competing command-system, trying to redirect worship and obedience. Abductive move: the packet supplies false authority and the later obedience prohibition; I infer that the human's transgression is institutional/commanding, not only private pride. Reading change: the ayah introduces a rival lordship pattern.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B001","root_000937/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8971869f012adaccde3c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:6:4\",\"root_000937\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sınır aşımı, insana verilmiş imkânların kendi ölçüsünü aşan su gibi taşması ve yaratılmış ölçüyü bozmasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a61ebdbb990c55ff9433","text":"2. **Transgression materializing as rival authority.** `ط غ ي:B003` supplies a false authority or deviant leader who turns others from good. In 96:9–10, the overstepping human becomes one who restrains a servant at prayer, and 96:11 places the servant on guidance. Those later roles give the branch a social function: the transgressor does not merely exceed a private limit but installs himself as a gatekeeper over another’s devotion. I infer the identity across the continuous sequence. The general statement therefore acquires an institutional reading: excess hardens into unauthorized command.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
